2026-10-20T01:54:28.5938461Z env:
2026-10-20T01:54:28.5938850Z   UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/uv-python-dir
2026-10-20T01:54:28.5939395Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-20T01:54:28.5956040Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Build and serve the Prefill MegaMoE combine pre-reduction (DeepGEMM host rebuild + header overlay)

Why: kernel benches 39217809231, 39332619830 and 39352299232 showed that
after per-128 FC2 scales the Prefill MegaMoE FC2 phase is bound by the
NVLink combine itself: every FC2 output row (token x top-k slot, 8 KB BF16,
about 201 MB per rank to remote ranks) is scattered to the token's rank,
and neither spreading FC2 over the FC1 phase (+8%) nor asynchronous bulk
copies (+3.2% balanced, -4.8% when nothing crosses NVLink) shortened the
160-200 us of scatter per CTA. The remaining lever is to send fewer bytes.

What: the expert rank sums the top-k-weighted FC2 outputs of a token's
experts that live on that rank in FP32 and sends one BF16 row per (token,
expert rank) instead of one per (token, top-k slot): remote combine bytes
about 201 -> 91 MB per rank. The weights are folded into the FC2 FP32
accumulator scale before the single BF16 conversion; rows with one local
expert go straight to the owner, rows with several are staged locally
(BF16) with an acq_rel arrival counter per (owner, token, 128-column
slice), and the warp that lands the last row sums the staged rows in slot
order in FP32 (deterministic), rounds once to BF16 and sends it. The owner
then reads at most four rank slots per token. Each rank publishes a mode
word so a (source, owner) pair pre-reduces only when both run the Prefill
geometry; mixed calls keep the raw path. No new waits: completion rides on
the existing grid sync and NVLink barrier (release.sys); counters return to
zero, the mode word is cleared by SM 0 at cleanup. Rounding: weighted rows
-> per-rank FP32 partial sum -> BF16 -> FP32 sum at the owner, i.e. order
and rounding changes only (allowed; FP8 operands, FP32 scales and
accumulation unchanged). Precision rule: github.com/hpcac/2026-APAC-HPC-AI.

How it is built and switched on:
- patches/deep-gemm-host/deep-gemm-host-pre-reduce.patch (series): the
  symmetric buffer gains four regions behind the combine buffer (mode 128
  B, counters 2.2 MB, reduced rows 138 MB, staging 1107 MB; about 1.25 GB
  per GPU at 4096 tokens per rank) and the generated JIT source defines
  DG_SM90_MEGA_MOE_PRE_REDUCE; DG_SM90_PRE_REDUCE=0 restores the legacy
  buffer and kernel. The image rebuilds only _C.so from v0.1.5 (1c8138a5)
  with the host series: containers/rebuild-deepgemm-host.py and its wiring
  (Dockerfile, build-sglang-container.sh, build-sm90-megamoe.py host record,
  check-firmus-image.sh host checks, tests) come back from d2fd691 without
  its SwapAB-144 host patch and header guard.
- patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-prefill-pre-reduce.patch is appended to
  deep_gemm_overlay_patches after sf128 and the swizzle (runtime header
  overlay, main 9861336); the image's header series is unchanged.
- sglang.sbatch exports DG_SM90_PRE_REDUCE=0 for Decode workers (Decode
  never pre-reduces, and the extra 1.25 GB would come out of its KV pool).
- scripts/check-firmus-megamoe.py: the reference follows each rank's
  geometry and the pair-wise rounding; three mixed-geometry cases and a
  routes case with 1-8 local experts per token were added; bounds
  unchanged. The gate runs with pre-reduction on, so it covers Prefill and
  Decode geometries in one call.

Checks: host patch applies to v0.1.5 1c8138a5 and csrc/tvm_ffi_api.cpp
passes g++ -fsyntax-only (link/load happen in the image build, which
imports the module against the CUDA stub); header chain (series + sf128 +
swizzle + pre-reduce) compiles with the image's CUDA 13.0 on Firmus:
Prefill 96 registers, spill 112/128 B (control 20/20 B; two reloads inside
the FC2 K loop), Decode SwapAB and non-swap SASS identical to control; a
Python protocol model covered 192 configurations (mixed geometry, 1-8
local experts, masked slots) with counters back at zero, no double send,
no unwritten read. pytest and ruff pass.

Expected: -40 to -100 us per Prefill MegaMoE call (2-5%), uncertain: the
staging writes (233 MB), reads (147 MB), counter atomics and the extra
spills can eat the saving. Implemented by a Sonnet agent and reviewed here.
The asynchronous bulk-copy scatter (39352299232) is not included.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
